GOVERNING REQ: `REQ_OFFER_MECHANISM__…__v20260806_1625.md` §12 step 6 — **R15** explicit response, **R16** exact-scope set equality, **R17** principal/authority validation, with **R18**'s integrity tie-in. **Nothing ruled MET; fixtures only, nothing presents to a real member, nothing enabled.** Built `harness/offer_response.py` — the only path by which an offer resolves and the only path by which scope changes — plus `eval/test_offer_response.py` (**32 tests**). **FOUR THINGS DELIBERATELY NOT BUILT:** a second state machine, its own event log, spent-ness logic (all three are HA-08's `spend_ledger`, durable with a process-kill proof — **a second would be a second source of truth about whether an offer is resolved**), and free-text parsing. **R17 IS ENFORCED BY THE ABSENCE OF A PARAMETER:** `apply_response` has no `role`, `is_owner`, `is_caregiver` or `has_prior_access` — only `authorities`, a list of explicit `DecisionAuthority` grants — so *"household role, account ownership, caregiver status, or prior access alone SHALL NOT substitute for decision authority"* holds **by construction rather than by a check that could be bypassed**, and a signature test keeps it that way so adding such a parameter later breaks the battery instead of quietly weakening R17. **A wildcard decision domain is refused at construction** (`*`/`all`/`any`) — *"authorized for everything" is "prior access alone" wearing a different hat*, and R17's "this exact decision domain" rules it out. **R15: THE RESPONSE ARRIVES CLASSIFIED, NEVER AS PROSE.** `ResponseKind` is a closed enum of eleven members, **nine of which exist only to be refused** — an enum with just ACCEPT/DECLINE would be shorter and worse, because every other case would fall into "unhandled", and unhandled is where silent acceptance lives. `apply_response` never sees the member's words: R15 forbids reinterpreting a response, and prose here would reintroduce the generative surface HA-22 just removed. The neutral instruction is a **CONSTANT, not a template** — anything parameterised could be personalised, and a personalised re-prompt is asking again. **R16: SET EQUALITY EVALUATED, not inspected** — scope is dimension-namespaced tokens so `scope_after == scope_before | delta` is checkable directly; **empty delta dimensions produce NO token** (§2.4: an omitted field means no change, and a token for it would grant what the offer never named — R16's "nothing more"); `action:notify` and `initiation:notify` are two grants, not one. **FOUR FAULT TWINS EXECUTED, ANTI-VACUITY FIRST** (every refusal is worthless on a module that refuses everything): (d) the principal's explicit yes **grants exactly the delta**, manifest shows `exact_set_equality: True`; (a) wrong member **REFUSED and RECORDED**, state stays PRESENTED; (b) accept against a tampered instance **INVALIDATES and grants nothing**; (c) *"whatever you think"* **resolves nothing** — and (c) is parametrized over EVERY non-resolving enum member so a future kind cannot be added untested. **THE ORDERING IS A DECISION, NOT AN ACCIDENT: R18 runs BEFORE the responder is examined**, because a wrong-member response to a *corrupted* offer must report the corruption, not the responder — reporting "not authorized" would leave tampering undetected and the record actively misleading; the two checks pass in either order and only one tells the truth, so the order is tested. **R18 invalidates the OFFER, not merely the grant** — terminal state, never re-presentable (R8). **THE OFFER-PATH TRAP HA-22 PREDICTED, CAUGHT:** its handoff note said a new offer-path module must be added to `OFFER_PATH_ENTRY_MODULES` "or it is simply not scanned" — checked, and `harness.offer_response` was indeed invisible (scanned: False, 10 modules) until declared (**PURE, 11 modules**). The one failure mode that check cannot self-detect. Battery refused before registration, passing after. **RIDER: `REQ_HARNESS_RUNNER` AMENDED** with HA-24's exit-code rule citing Bill's 2026-08-09 ruling verbatim — no other REQ text changed, status stays MET — which also answers HA-24's own open governance finding that the runner's exit semantics had no REQ home. Any binding failure -> non-zero." **WHY IT WAS NEEDED:** item 12's amendment (HA-20) made live-model layers reported rather than gating, **but the exit code kept failing on them** — so HA-20 and HA-22 each had to write "read the layer lines, not the exit code," a standing instruction to ignore exit codes that is corrosive well beyond this script. **An exit code that does not mean what the rule means is worse than no exit code.** **THE CLASSIFICATION LIVES IN `eval/harnesslib/reporter.py`, NOT IN SHELL** — that is where a scenario's layer is known, and parsing layer names back out of stdout in zsh would be a second, drifting copy of the rule; `run_harness.sh` carries the documented contract and propagates the status via `pipefail`. **Severity order unchanged** (regression still outranks brand-new, both still printed in full, exit 1 vs 2 preserved) — only *which* failures gate is new. **Bill's warning line verified BYTE-FOR-BYTE by `diff` against the ruling, not by eye**, since anything reading stdout for that string reads a fixed contract. **UNKNOWN LAYER KEYS DEFAULT TO BINDING** (`L9:brand_new_layer` and a malformed key both gate) — the opposite default fails silently: one typo in `LIVE_LAYERS` would quietly stop a whole layer from being able to fail the build and nothing would look wrong, the same shape as HA-22's non-existent-forbidden-module trap. **ALL THREE CASES EXECUTED: (a)** `--full`, binding green (L7 27/27, L7V2 27/28, AUDIT 9/9, DISC/SCHEMA/VOICE 1/1) with live reds `L2:routing_showcase.T04` + `L1:P12` -> **EXIT 0 and the line printed, naming both failures rather than replacing them**; **(b)** a planted stray key turning the binding `AUDIT:KEY-HYGIENE-ZERO-ORPHAN` red -> **EXIT 2, and the reassuring line correctly ABSENT** (a warning that printed unconditionally would be worse than none); **(c)** `--layer 7`, all binding -> **EXIT 0, RATCHET PASS, warning line count 0**. **THE SCRATCH BREAK EDITED NO SOURCE FILE** — it planted one key in `~/hip-keys/` and destroyed it after, so "restored byte-for-byte" is trivially true and a crash mid-run could not have left a broken tree. **ITEM 3 — NOTHING CONSUMES THIS EXIT CODE, enumerated not assumed:** no CI, no `Makefile`, no active git hooks; `ceiling_status.py` reads `run_harness.sh` as **TEXT** to parse the battery file-list, never its status; `gate_check.sh` runs routing/injection/integration harnesses, different scripts; two other scripts mention it in comments only. **The only consumer was a human reading a dispatch — which is exactly what this fixes.** RUNS: batteries **864 passed / 0 failed**, L7 27/27, AUDIT 9/9, **RATCHET PASS**, memory **13/17 inside the pin**. **C-11 DELIBERATELY DID NOT MOVE:** its PARTIAL rests on the live layers having no reproducibility rule, and making them non-gating does not give them one — reading this as progress on C-11 would be the exact mistake the CLAIM IMPACT line exists to prevent. **GOVERNANCE GAP REPORTED, NOT PAPERED OVER: this dispatch changed code and named no REQ, so Requirements Discipline item 8's gate FIRED.** `REQ_HARNESS_RUNNER` (MET) governs the runner's **preconditions and refusals**, not ratchet exit semantics; the governing standard is CLAUDE.md item 12 as amended at HA-20 — arguably stronger than a REQ, but not a REQ doc. **No retroactive REQ was written; item 8 forbids exactly that.** The build completed rather than refused because the dispatch carried a verbatim rule and an executable three-case acceptance test — whether that suffices is Bill's call. | HA-22: offer step 4 — no generative surface, proven over the import closure | dispatches/DISPATCH_HA22_OFFER_STEP4__no-generative-surface-closure-checked-model-client-removed__v20260809_2115.md | BUILT | HA-22, 2026-08-09. TYPE: BUILD. GOVERNING REQ: `REQ_OFFER_MECHANISM__…__v20260806_1625.md` §12 step 4, R12/R13. **Nothing ruled MET; fixtures only, nothing presents to a real member.** **THE CHECK FOUND A REAL GENERATIVE REACH ON ITS FIRST EXECUTION.** The offer path imported `harness.extraction_queue` — the module carrying the **Groq/Ollama fact detector** — via `harness.inference_permit` and `harness.write_origins`, **two hops below `harness/offer_instance.py`, the one file HA-06's scans cover.** **Was it a model CALL? No** — both imports pull one name, `CANONICAL_ATTRIBUTES`, a dict of 17 strings, and nothing ever called the detector. **It was removed anyway, and the reasoning is the point: step 4 requires that no model call CAN enter the path, and "imported but never called" is a fact about today's code that no structural check can keep true tomorrow** — importing the module executes its body and leaves the client one attribute access away. **REMOVAL: `harness/attribute_vocabulary.py`, a LEAF module that imports nothing at all**; `extraction_queue` re-exports the name so **all twelve original call sites are unchanged**, verified as the *same object* through both names (`is` → True, 17 entries). A battery test asserts the leaf stays a leaf — **any import added there is inherited by the offer path**, the one way this fix silently un-fixes itself. **BUILT `harness/offer_purity.py`: a breadth-first IMPORT-CLOSURE check by AST** from five entry modules, forbidding nine first-party model/interpreter/generation modules, sixteen third-party+stdlib surfaces (**including `random`/`secrets` for R13's "randomization input" and `importlib` because a computed dynamic import would defeat a static closure**), and ten generative call targets. **WHY AST NOT REGEX, with fresh evidence from this build (D-75):** a regex for `generate(` returns **three confident false positives** — `X25519PrivateKey.generate()` in `member_seal_keys`/`household_keys`/`dyad_crypto`, key generation not text generation — and **a substring scan of `offer_purity.py` would fail against itself**, since its prose necessarily contains every word it forbids. **TWO FAULT TWINS EXECUTED: (A)** `call_frontier` injected directly into `offer_instance` → RED; **(B) the case that matters — `memory_engine.interpreter` injected into `purpose_trigger` TWO HOPS DOWN, leaving `offer_instance.py` clean → RED naming the module, while HA-06's single-file scan shape run against the SAME injected fault reports "NOTHING — it passes."** That contrast is the whole justification for step 4 being its own build: the existing scan is not wrong, it is not deep enough, and the difference is invisible until something is injected below it. Restored and green, `git diff --stat` confirming no residual change. **THREE ANTI-VACUITY REFUSALS, each closing a way a purity check reports green while enforcing nothing:** zero modules scanned is a refusal, not a pass; **a closure that does not expand past the entry set is a refusal** (these modules import one another, so a flat closure means imports are not being followed — exactly the bug that hides a deep call, and it looks identical to success); and **every forbidden first-party module must EXIST**, because a rename or typo can never match and would disarm the guard silently forever. The report names every module and file it scanned. **The manifest mechanism caught this dispatch's own battery** before registration (`assert not ['test_offer_purity.py']`) — HA-03's self-registration rule working on its author. RUNS: batteries **864 passed / 0 failed** (851→864), L7 **27/27**, L7V2 27/28, AUDIT **9/9**, **RATCHET PASS**, `KEY-HYGIENE-ZERO-ORPHAN` PASS, memory harness **13/17 inside the pin**; `--full` live layers logged to the collector, reported with **no gate claim**. **DOES NOT CLAIM:** a dynamic guarantee (the closure is static — stated in the module's own docstring, not hidden); anything about the rest of the system (`memory_engine.store`, `fact_change`, `write_rule` all legitimately reach forbidden surfaces and serve as the battery's fault twins); and **nothing MET** — A10 and A1-A20 unattempted, REQ still DRAFT-RATIFIED-PENDING. **CLAIM IMPACT: C-07** — its second clause ("no generative surface exists in the offer path") has standing evidence for the first time; the first clause is HA-06's, untouched. | HA-20: harness hygiene and the item-12 split — invariant relocated, teardown wired, deterministic/live separated | dispatches/DISPATCH_HA20_HYGIENE_AND_ITEM12__invariant-relocated-teardown-wired-item12-split__v20260809_2014.md | BUILT | HA-20, 2026-08-09. TYPE: BUILD + GOVERNANCE. **CLAIM IMPACT: C-10, C-11 — named, not ruled.** **ITEM 1 — BILL'S RULINGS RECORDED: `REQ_DERIVED_WRITE_CUSTODY` is MET** (ruled on HA-19's acceptance table), **and the rule-3a subject-keyed scope fix is RATIFIED in his words — *"Scope follows the subject, not the author. Keep the fix."*** HA-19 had flagged it as its one judgement call and offered to revert; it stands as a principle rather than a session's reading of an outcome. Both the REQ header and HA-19's dispatch doc carry the ruling, and both state what it does NOT cover (HA-19's ratchet reds; `encode()`'s silent no-op; no persisted `author` property). **ITEM 2 — ITEM 12 IS SPLIT, verbatim from Bill:** deterministic tests (batteries, L7, L7V2, AUDIT, DISC, SCHEMA, VOICE and the ratchet over them) **remain binding and must pass every time**; live-model tests are **reported separately and do not gate** until a reproducibility rule exists; **no best-of-N, no invented pass threshold — collect runs first, set the rule from data.** The amendment records WHY (HA-19's three `--full` runs, the last two byte-identical and disagreeing on L1/L3/L4/L6 and on the regression list itself) and closes the obvious loophole: **hiding a live-layer red because it "doesn't gate" breaks the rule as surely as claiming a green ratchet.** **ITEM 3 — THE COLLECTOR BUILT:** `logs/harness/live_layer_results.csv`, appended AUTOMATICALLY by every `--full` from `eval/harness.py`, one row **per live scenario** (`run_id, ts, commit, mode, layer, sid, status, tier`). Per-test, not per-layer, deliberately — "L3 went 3/3 → 1/3" cannot distinguish one chronically unstable test from three occasionally unstable ones. **No threshold is implemented anywhere; that is the point.** **ITEM 4 — THE ZERO-ORPHAN INVARIANT RELOCATED** out of the pytest battery into `eval/harness.py` as `AUDIT:KEY-HYGIENE-ZERO-ORPHAN` (tier ABSOLUTE), running after every layer including L7. It had been asserting its postcondition BEFORE the biggest key producers ran — and since `run_harness.sh` gates the harness behind the batteries, that is why every `--full` left keys behind and the next one aborted at the gate. **PROVEN BOTH DIRECTIONS: green on a clean tree; RED naming the offender (`1 leaked: ['_snd_ha20strayproof.seal.key']`, AUDIT 8/9, exit 2) with a surviving stray; green again once destroyed.** **A FALSE ALL-CLEAR WAS CAUGHT IN THE PROOF ITSELF AND IS RECORDED:** the first attempt planted the key mid-layer-7 via a log-watcher, raced the buffered output, landed after the check, and reported PASS — which would have read as the proof. A mid-layer key is destroyed by teardown (correct); to exercise the invariant the key must SURVIVE teardown, so a pre-existing key is the deterministic and real-world-faithful plant. **ITEM 5 — TEARDOWN WIRED** into both layer-7 entrypoints in a `finally` (a raising probe must still clean up, or one bad run poisons the next one's gate), destroying **the delta** rather than the directory so a concurrent lane's keys are untouched; P4 keeps its own `_ensure_member` but feeds the ONE teardown path via `register_for_teardown`. **A FIFTH PRODUCER FOUND — `ctxstrip_probe_owner` — which the classifier had been FAILING CLOSED on and silently keeping since that probe was written; found because the relocated check REPORTS unclassified keys rather than passing over them.** **ITEM 5's SECOND HALF WAS BUILT AND BACKED OUT, and that is a finding, not a skip:** provisioning a household wrap requires enrolling the probe as a household CIRCLE member, which makes it a **permanent participant in the household key tree** — and teardown destroys its key, so `ensure_household_keys` dies (`FileNotFoundError: ob4_probe_owner.seal.key`) and **every household-circle-shared write breaks, the real demo's included. Item 5's two halves are in direct conflict: an ephemeral principal cannot also be a permanent custodian.** The log noise stays as the cheaper problem; the real fix is in `read_user_facts` and is Bill's call. Damage repaired in-session (circle roster restored to bill/maya/sam, stale wrap dropped). **A SUBTLER TRAP EXPOSED AND FIXED AT BOTH ENDS:** destroying a key while leaving its registered `seal_pubkey` creates a silent desync — fresh private key, stale pubkey, and **the probe's own fact becomes unreadable to itself, reported as "fact not present", never as a key error.** It took L7:PSA1 and L7V2:SC1 red mid-build. **KNOWN BROKEN, NOT FIXED: `household_keys.remove_circle_member` raises `sqlite3.OperationalError: no such column: epoch`** — circle removal does not work today; out of scope for a hygiene dispatch. RUNS: batteries **851 passed / 0 failed**, L7 **27/27**, L7V2 27/28, AUDIT **9/9**, **RATCHET PASS**, memory harness **13/17 inside the pin**. **`--full` run TWICE BACK TO BACK with no hand cleanup** — see the doc §6 for both results and the live-layer split. Symlink LATEST_DISPATCH_HA20_HYGIENE_AND_ITEM12.md |

| D-162: completion alert becomes a script; its limits recorded in the rule | dispatches/DISPATCH_COMPLETION_ALERT__script-owns-the-sound-and-its-limits__v20260804_1123.md + scripts/dispatch_done.sh | BUILT | D-162, 2026-08-04. **DOCS + one small script; REQ: NONE; nothing ruled.** PROBLEM: a session reads CLAUDE.md once at startup and then works from memory, so a rule edit reaches only sessions starting afterwards — which is why D-153 was silent while D-149/D-150 rang. BUILT: scripts/dispatch_done.sh <complete|findings|stopped>, **silent on success** (the sound is the whole output), and on failure one stderr line + non-zero exit — **2 for usage error, 3 for environment failure** (afplay absent / sound unreadable / playback failed) so a session can tell 'I called it wrong' from 'the machine could not ring' and report the second honestly. All paths verified: three outcomes exit 0 with empty stdout AND stderr; no-arg, bad-arg and two-arg each exit 2 with one stderr line. RULE: STANDARD PREAMBLE item 6 now names the COMMAND, not the sound files — the script owns which sound, how many times, and the failure handling, so those can change without any open session relearning anything. **LIMITS WRITTEN INTO THE RULE ITSELF, not a footnote: a session that never calls it rings nothing (nothing enforces the call — the pre-D-146 lock shape); a session that dies mid-run rings nothing; a session that began BEFORE the rule landed does not know to call it at all — naming the script fixes the cost of changing the SOUND, NOT the staleness of the RULE; therefore SILENCE IS NOT PROOF OF PROGRESS — running, stuck, finished and failed are all silent, and the report is the evidence.** Durable fix NAMED not built: a harness-level stop hook, the only shape that survives session age because it depends on nothing a session remembers. Numbering confirmed against the log (last logged D-160; another lane runs a concurrent D-R-161 sequence). Stale D-161 worktree cleared first |

THE FAULT TWIN CAUGHT A FLAW IN THE TEST ITSELF: the transitivity case was first written with a single-parent chain and the twin PASSED it, because with one parent 'any parent gone' and 'all parents gone' coincide -- it discriminated nothing and would have xfailed vacuously. Rewritten with a surviving second parent. This is the argument for asserting the twin is STILL BROKEN before calling pytest.xfail; without it the vacuity ships looking green. HARNESS DISCIPLINE all four covered, with the metamorphic wrapper NOT a rewording wrapper (nothing here consumes an utterance) but an ORDER-INVARIANCE wrapper over both the lineage list and the retracted-root list -- if either changed the outcome, a child's fate would rest on write order. KNOWN LIMIT STATED NOT BURIED: the battery runs a SIMULATED graph, because testing a missing cascade needs multi-level lineage, mixed retracted/active parents and a cycle on demand -- against live Neo4j that means mutating the frozen demo graph (forbidden) or a throwaway DB (unavailable), and a battery that silently skips without a DB cannot be shown RED on command, which REQ_CEILING_ACCEPTANCE names as disqualifying. Also read docs/design/DESIGN_EARNED_CALIBRATION per instruction 1 — its scope line (a confidence measure may gate INFERENTIAL RECOVERY only, never collection depth) binds any future build here but was not itself exercised, since the dispatch stopped before implementation. **SURVEY (a): `cascade_recompute_eligible` is set in exactly one place**, `harness/derivation_cascade.py:130-131` inside `cascade_from_parents()`, `eligible = len(alive) >= MIN_PARENTS_FOR_RECOMPUTE` (`:123`) — nothing reads either field today, zero hits by grep. To be consumed it would need a later pass querying closed facts with the flag set, a call back into the SAME abstraction path scoped to the surviving parents, a new `:Fact` via `create_fact_node` on success, and no action on failure (the child is already correctly invalidated either way). **(b) STOP CONDITION FIRED, exactly as the dispatch anticipated it might.** The original derivation is not a pure function of stored data — `memory_engine/consolidate.py::_abstract_pass` calls `interpreter.abstract(episodes)`, and the real production interpreter, `memory_engine/interpreter.py::GroqInterpreter`, is a live Groq (Llama 4 Scout) call returning zero or more facts. `derivation_cascade.py`'s OWN docstring, written at D-81 before this dispatch existed, already said as much ("Re-deriving a consolidated fact requires an LLM abstraction call") — this survey confirms that sentence describes an architectural fact, not a timing caution: **recompute as R18's text reads ("recompute child and replace lineage") cannot be built as a mechanical operation on stored evidence; any real implementation is a second, independent, non-deterministic model call that may return a different fact, or none.** That is a different requirement than the one filed, and per instruction this needed Bill's ruling, not an implementation — none was attempted. **(c) The evidence floor and the one-surviving-parent case:** `MIN_PARENTS_FOR_RECOMPUTE = 2` is the SAME constant `_abstract_pass` itself enforces (`len(episodes) < 2: continue`) — one shared number, not two independently chosen ones. One surviving parent after a retraction is a **plain invalidate** (`1 >= 2` is False) — never a recompute attempt that then fails its own floor; the floor gates ELIGIBILITY before any recompute would be tried, so that failure mode doesn't exist in the current design. **(d) Trust level: not preserved, because nothing is preserved from.** `_abstract_pass` unconditionally clamps every derived fact to `confidence="low"` regardless of its inputs' confidence — a recomputed fact would go through the identical clamp, matching R18's own "replace," not "inherit." **TD-139/TD-141: explicitly out of scope, confirmed unaffected** — TD-139 concerns what gets recorded about a derivation (untouched by whether recompute is attempted), TD-141 is a data-state finding about the live graph's one derived fact (untouched by a code-path question). **Steps 3-5 (implement, TD-139/141 re-check via code, harness re-run) not reached** — no code changed, so `--layer 7`/RATCHET/memory harness were not re-run; nothing to verify a delta against, and the git diff (docs-only) shows that directly. **OPEN, recorded for whoever picks this next:** three shapes a ruling could take — amend R18's text to describe what recompute actually is (a fresh, possibly-disagreeing derivation attempt); build it as specified anyway, accepting the non-determinism and an open question about whether every eligible child recomputes unconditionally or needs its own gate; or leave TD-140 open and R18 NOT MET indefinitely on the basis that invalidate-only already errs safe and a model-call-dependent recompute is a big enough behavior change to need its own REQ first. No recommendation made between the three — A18 still passes, R18 stays NOT MET, unchanged by this dispatch |

Reminder carried: **the interval IS the detection window.** **ACCEPTANCE, all four with fault twins** (eval/test_anchor_emitter.py, 21 cases, 13th standing battery): an emitted anchor matches the head (twin: a stale head_hash MUST fail); the emitter refuses to overwrite (twin: a plain open(path,'w') MUST clobber, proving the refusal does work); the anchor leaks nothing in **record AND filename** (twin: a payload-bearing record is refused BEFORE it lands); and a crypto-shred does not invalidate an emitted anchor (**twin: a rewrite after the anchor MUST be detected — the discriminating one, since 'survives a lawful shred' is only meaningful if something does NOT, else the criterion is satisfied by an anchor that survives tampering too**). ANTI-VACUITY throughout, including the sharpest: the leak test would pass against an anchor leaking everything if the payloads had never held the canaries, so their presence is proven. **ONE BEHAVIOR FOUND BY EXERCISING THE MANUAL PATH, CHECKED AND KEPT RATHER THAN DEDUPED:** running the emitter again with an unmoved head writes a SECOND file for the same seq differing only in anchored_at. That is not clutter — **two anchors of an unchanged head attest the head did not move across that interval, ruling out both appends AND rewrites in between**; deduplicating by seq would discard that evidence. The only refusal is a same-second collision, where the records would be byte-identical. Plus: raw recall query text is a **separate defect, fixed regardless of the cascade** (TD-R-173); committed synthetic fixtures are a different class and outside the erasure boundary; and an acceptance definition broader than database deletion, with three outcomes (ERASED / OPAQUE_PROOF_REMAINS / OUTSIDE_ERASURE_BOUNDARY) where **a fourth implicit "we forgot about that store" bucket FAILS acceptance.** **THE GATE: no real-data erasure until BOTH key-custody cleanup AND the semantic-metadata cascade land** — written into `HIP_HANDOFF.md` CURRENT STATE by HA-13 |

| ChatGPT research pass 3 — trust-axis evaluation (BANKED VERBATIM) | reviews/CHATGPT_research-pass3-trust-axis-evaluation.txt | BANKED | Source: ChatGPT, prompted by Bill. Committed D-60, 2026-07-31 (present on disk since ~D-49 but NEVER COMMITTED until now — see the correction note on the REQ_TRUST_AXES row). 1,166 lines, verbatim, no header block (matching the pass1/pass2 convention). THE DIRECT BASIS FOR BILL'S D-53 'record both, rank neither' RULING: it opens by stating there is 'no defensible canonical total ordering of observed < asserted < corroborated < confirmed', and that there is no established general rule for when a conversational agent should re-approach after withdrawal (the literature supports topic-specific backoff, contextual timing, explicit permission and attempt limits — not autonomous persistence). Three fundamental problems named up front: (1) 'confirmed' conflates truth, consent, authority and provenance — a person can confirm a false fact, refuse to confirm a true one, be coerced, or lack authority over a relational fact; (2) 'recognize and route, never adjudicate' is too passive for imminent danger — avoiding a truth verdict is right, avoiding a risk assessment is not; (3) strict per-member isolation is not currently true — with operator-controlled hardware and no per-member keys it is POLICY isolation, not cryptographic isolation. Argues confirmation should not be modeled as unconfirmed-fact -> confirmed-fact at all but as an ATTRIBUTED CLAIM preserving both perspectives without merging them — the position D-50's portrait model independently reached. ALL CLAIMS UNVERIFIED, proposes no REQ |
| Dimensioned-ceiling axes: candidate content, TWO SOURCES in one file (BANKED VERBATIM) | reviews/D63_dimensioned-ceiling-axes__fable-and-chatgpt__v20260731_1917.md | BANKED | D-63 work, banked D-64, 2026-07-31. Carries TWO independent passes on the same question, verbatim, unmerged and unreconciled — where they disagree, both readings stand as filed. Source 1 = FABLE codebase pass, lines 42-319, read-only at HEAD 9acc5a2, claims marked [code-verified] checked against the repo. Source 2 = CHATGPT literature pass, lines 321-1226. Body verified byte-identical to the source. UNVERIFIED; NO REQ proposed; changes no design and no status. Subject: candidate content for the DIMENSIONED structural ceiling Bill ruled for (categories, retention, audience, inferential reach) which engagement cannot raise; scalar depth stays UNSET and instrument-blocked, 'Follow engagement' and all three depth-earning justifications dead. FABLE PASS, headline findings: (i) HIP ALREADY HAS the categories mechanism, ratified — risk_pattern is deliberately kept out of CANONICAL_ATTRIBUTES (Bill 2026-07-17, REQ_D21_D23) so 'a detector that classifies live utterances should not be able to emit a fact type nobody says out loud'; the axis needs that generalized, not a parallel mechanism. (ii) SELF-CORRECTION of D-61: interpreter.py:388-392 sends the abstraction model attribute + confidence ONLY, never values, so inference is metadata-over-metadata and materially narrower than D-61 implied. (1) CATEGORIES: HIP's vocabulary is ATTRIBUTE-shaped while the categories worth refusing are SUBJECT-shaped, and they do not line up — there is no 'cognition' attribute to refuse because cognitive facts are already collectable under health_condition and incident, so refusing cognition means carving a subject out of two attributes in active use, which needs a value-level test the enum cannot express and D-50 Principle 6 (content-blindness) forbids. The only cheap refusals are the categories eldercare does not need. Better-shaped candidate: restrict by WRITE PATH, as risk_pattern already is. (2) RETENTION: mostly theatre today. valid_to tombstoning keeps row, ciphertext, DEK and embedding; tier demotion deletes nothing; and there is exactly ONE hard delete in the codebase (server/demo_dashboard.py:1890, nuke-the-graph demo reset) with NO per-fact/per-member/per-category delete call site anywhere. Live narrow leak named: embeddings over '{owner} {attribute}' survive retraction, so the existence and shape of a retracted fact persists (the value never entered the vector, TD-030 holds). The append-only ledger makes part of this STRUCTURAL — two ratified goals in conflict, needing a ruling not a build. (3) AUDIENCE: a ceiling can narrow who ELSE reads a claim but CANNOT narrow out the author, because INJ-3's fact.owner == requester permit is backed by the author holding the DEK wrap — removing their access is a re-encryption, not a policy edit, the same shape as Part 5's account-holder-is-abuser limit. Defensible candidate is a cap on PROPAGATION; cheapest axis because it is preventive. (4) INFERENTIAL REACH: four real constraints already exist (trust cap, >=2 sources, same owner/subject, metadata-only input) but SUBJECT MATTER is wide open — consolidate.py never validates df.attribute against CANONICAL_ATTRIBUTES, which is enforced only on the extraction path (extraction_queue.py:229, :903), so abstract() may mint any attribute string. A DERIVABLE_ATTRIBUTES allowlist is a small fix but introduces a SECOND vocabulary that must contain risk_pattern while excluding things CANONICAL_ATTRIBUTES contains — overlapping non-nested enums, the drift shape that produced the three contradictory trust orderings. EIGHT COLLISIONS surfaced not resolved, sharpest: REQ_CARE_TEAM_READ_AUTH deliberately WIDENED audience so any audience ceiling narrows that widening [**CORRECTED 2026-07-31 (D-71): this row and the banked Fable pass both originally said '(MET)'. That is FALSE.** The REQ is NOT MET -- its own header :2, its INDEX status column, and no MET block anywhere in it -- and its five-row acceptance has no executable form (D-68: no runner references it, and its fixtures do not exist, both care-team tables being empty so the INJ-3 permit cannot fire at all). The widening IS live in code (injection_contract.py:515-518), but under an unruled REQ with an unrunnable acceptance -- so the collision is 'a ceiling narrows an unproven widening already governing live reads', not 'a ceiling narrows what a MET REQ deliberately opened'. The substance stands; its character changes. A CORRECTIONS section was APPENDED to the banked artifact rather than rewriting either pass. See also TD-138 (D-69), an epoch-blindness defect in the same code path.]; REQ_CURATOR_SHADOW_SCORER (MET) asserts its ten-key feature space EXACTLY at ABSOLUTE tier so touching the attribute space or keying a ceiling on sensitivity inherits the still-live 'critical' misranking; and D-50 Principle 6 is a collision with a PRINCIPLE rather than a REQ, which is what makes the categories axis hard. Fable's recommendation: build axes 3 and 4, state 1 and 2 as limits rather than mechanisms. CHATGPT PASS covers the same four axes and adds a FIFTH section the Fable pass did not scope — OFFER GOVERNANCE (offers purpose-triggered not engagement-triggered; no adaptive persuasion; decline creates a presumption against repetition; acceptance rate is not a product KPI) — which is the gap D-61 identified without proposing content for. It also argues topic-based prohibitions work poorly, proposes a defensible hard-prohibition list plus restricted-rather-than-prohibited categories, treats the cognition collision directly, argues append-only audit and erasure are compatible only if the ledger does not contain the personal data, separates four rights on the audience axis, and holds that 'the author always gets permanent readback' is not defensible — a direct disagreement with what the Fable pass found HIP currently enforces |

The security risk is co-residency, not that endpoint: any public reachability of that origin also exposes /api/facts, /api/decrypt, and the graph-nuking path. Estimates: trace capture 2-3 dispatch-days, replay player 4-6, mode-2 proxy 5-8 and gated. Cut line recommended: ship REPLAY as v1 (a recording has no live attack surface), hold mode 2 |

| Fable critique of the progressive-authorization answer, against HIP (BANKED VERBATIM) | reviews/FABLE_D61_critique__progressive-authorization__v20260731_1831.md | BANKED | D-61 review, banked D-62, 2026-07-31. Reviewer: Fable. Subject: the pass-4 answer's central claim that engagement may justify OFFERING a deeper capability but must never itself AUTHORIZE deeper collection ('earn depth' -> 'earn the opportunity to request a scoped depth grant'), critiqued against HIP specifically, against HIP_HouseholdSeeding_Roadmap v20260731_1351 and the codebase at af9e5b0. Verbatim; body verified byte-identical to the critique as produced. UNVERIFIED; NO REQ proposed; changes no design and no status. (a) The proposal SEVERS one edge and RELOCATES the rest: the structural ceiling cuts the loop at the point where accumulation converts into permission, but the grant machinery moves the problem to who issues the grant -- the same acquiescent user -- and the answer's own diagnosis says that user cannot be detected. Sharpest internal inconsistency: 'engagement may trigger an offer, never authorization' leaves OFFER RATE ungoverned, while the answer's own gaming section names offer-sequencing as an attack. Teach-back is a quality gate per grant; nothing bounds how often the system may ask. (b) HIP already has more than the answer assumes, code-verified: parked claims (store.py:462 -> confirmation_gate.apply_confirm/apply_decline) are already a scoped, per-item human authorization with a no-cost decline; the CONFIRMED rung already encodes 'authorization is a human act, never an inference'; and the hash-chained epistemic ledger ALREADY satisfies the answer's control item 5 (immutable event-level log of authorizations and withdrawals). Stated plainly: the injection contract is a READ-side gate and does nothing about collection, so HIP's strongest existing control does not transfer to this problem. (c) Passages that die: 'Follow engagement' outright ('a household that engages eagerly may earn depth faster' is the precise sentence the claim forbids); 'Earn depth' inverts, since all three of its named justifications (time in relationship, confirmed-fact count, unprompted volunteering) become impermissible; the absolute depth ceiling is PROMOTED from backstop to primary mechanism, making its currently-unset value the most consequential number in Part 3. The largest implied rebuild is in PART 1, not Part 3, and was not named in the dispatch: 'confirmation and depth are the same mechanism running in two directions' cannot survive grant semantics, because confirming an already-volunteered fact needs no grant and eliciting a new one does. (d) AGREES the latent states are not identifiable from free dialogue -- which invalidates the canary the reviewer's own Finding 5 proposed -- but finds the claim TOO STRONG for HIP's substrate, which is not only dialogue: apply_decline, outcome.kind (override = control_decline) and confirmed_by are structured decisions carrying a genuine no-cost refusal, and the answer's own signal table lists exactly that as useful while recommending within-person counterfactual testing as the least-bad fallback. Three limits kept honest: a member who never declines stays ambiguous; the P8 parked-claim sample fires on cross-principal conflict so it is narrow and non-random; and D-41 showed the correction path was structurally dead until D-42, so the history is thin. (e) INFERENCE: partially has it -- consolidate.py:435 makes derived facts always low-confidence and unable to harden without human confirmation, so inference cannot self-promote (the answer's rule already enforced), but there is NO category control on what may be inferred. REVOCATION: does not have it, and the codebase says so in its own words -- retract_fact closes rather than deletes, injection_contract.py:492-493 states revocation gives 'no new access, not unremembers ... the same limit as every other revocation in this codebase', and derived_from is WRITTEN BUT NEVER READ for invalidation, so retracting a source leaves its derived child standing (the answer's own scenario, live). The ledger's append-only hash chain makes part of this a genuine designed tension needing a ruling, not a build task. EDGE CONSENT: HIP has the OPPOSITE -- INJ-3 permits 'fact.owner == requester -- owner reads any fact they stored (any subject)', so the author of a claim about another person always retains access and the subject never enters the read path. (f) Overreach found: 'no collection metric can unlock additional collection', stated absolutely, would forbid REQ_CURATOR_SHADOW_SCORER's cold-start threshold -- a ratified, MET HIP design; the answer states a narrow version once and the broad version elsewhere, and only the narrow one is defensible. The regulatory section assumes a deployed multi-tenant consumer service when HIP is a single-household prototype on operator-custodial hardware. It treats the elder as the paradigm user when HIP's harder case is the dependent who cannot grant at all, running into its own unreconciled 'what if the caregiver is the threat'. And it recommends teach-back without costing it, in a voice-first system that already discovered via the custodian queue that per-item friction at volume produces habituation rather than attention. The diagnosis itself (acquiescence plus deferential vulnerability; absence of dissent misread as authorization) is judged CORRECT and better-named than Finding 5 |
| ChatGPT research pass 4 — engagement-as-authorization, acquiescence, progressive authorization (BANKED VERBATIM) | reviews/CHATGPT_research-pass4-engagement-authorization.txt | BANKED | Source: ChatGPT, prompted by Bill 2026-07-31. Subject: Finding 5 of the D-46 Fable critique (the depth loop bounded-but-not-decoupled, and the canary metrics being structurally blind to the population most at risk because all three require a negative signal). Captured verbatim and unedited; not summarized, not evaluated, proposes no REQ. ALL CLAIMS UNVERIFIED by this or any dispatch, per the docs/reviews/ convention. Argues the flaw is more fundamental than a missing tripwire: engagement is generated by multiple latent causes (enthusiasm, loneliness, deference, dependency, habitual acquiescence, impaired executive function, fear of losing assistance), several of which simultaneously raise answering and lower refusal -- so a refusal-dependent controller grants most depth to those least able to regulate it, and 'absence of resistance is not evidence of voluntary authorization'. Names the closest established constructs (Kipnis deferential vulnerability; Belmont impaired voluntariness / undue influence; acquiescence response style; Marlowe-Crowne and Paulhus social desirability; dementia-research assent/dissent asymmetry; FTC/EDPB deceptive design) while stating plainly that the exact architecture failure has no single canonical name. Reports a LITERATURE GAP directly relevant to Finding 5's proposed fix: no validated instrument infers from unrestricted conversation alone whether a disclosure reflects enthusiasm, politeness, loneliness, impression management, impairment, dependency or informed authorization -- the distinction is probably not identifiable from behavior alone, so elaboration rate and unprompted disclosure are engagement measures, not consent-validity measures. Recommends progressive authorization over detect-vs-cap: a structural ceiling engagement cannot raise, scoped/expiring/reversible depth grants, decision-specific safeguards (teach-back, neutral alternatives, delayed confirmation), detectors that may only pause or narrow, and no collection metric that can unlock collection. Also covers Campbell/Goodhart gaming (specific assistant-side and operator-side gaming routes), performative effects, and GDPR/EU AI Act/FTC/WA-MHMD/CA/CO regulatory exposure. NOT ACTED ON: this filing banks the source only -- Finding 5 remains open and is Bill's call, and nothing here changes the roadmap, any REQ, or any status |

Social attestation is the separate, unbuilt ATTESTED rung. (b) The justification/trust-ordering claim is now bounded by Bill's D-53 'record both, rank neither' ruling: justifications make inputs available to a consumer that has the context to weigh them; they do not license the system to compute a relative-confidence verdict. (c) Research-grounding appendix opening REWRITTEN -- the prior claim that external review 'repeatedly confirmed' the design lands inside established literatures was FALSE and is withdrawn; the four things the D-46 review actually found (progressive profiling reinvented uncited, motivational interviewing misappropriated, funnel technique cited for the wrong property, foot-in-the-door omitted) are now stated in the doc as open unresolved critique, citing the banked artifact so the paragraph can be checked against its source. (d) eligible_confirmers named a CURRENT BLOCKER rather than absorbed into durable-pending: all five registry rows have household_id = NULL and dyads has no member columns, so every member-owned fact lands durable-pending on day one because the system cannot derive who the confirmer is -- explicitly distinguished from genuine stranding, where the confirmer is unreachable. Also RETIRED the stale provenance note (the research passes ARE banked, D-49 + D-54/D-55), withdrawn rather than carried forward. LATEST symlink repointed. PRIOR HISTORY: five-part pre-spec roadmap, no code exists for any part: (1) Confirmation Subroutine — persistent always-on mechanism owning every fact's UNCONFIRMED->CONFIRMED journey by its authorized confirmer; never forces, never dead-ends, self-healing; also drives progressive graph-deepening (same decision, two directions); seeding/KYC/consolidation are CALLERS, not implementations; custodian confirms every dependent fact individually, no bulk shortcut, by design. (2) Seeding/Onboarding, a caller — one designated narrator, voice-first, progressive/resumable across sessions (no single-session requirement); four zones in order (household-with-pride -> people -> care/how-you-live -> optional), high-value-low-friction first, never-shame, reflect-don't-interrogate; MINIMUM SEED threshold left OPEN (Bill's call, same posture as REQ_CURATOR_SHADOW_SCORER's Gate A), degradation must be legible+invitational not silent; narrator-stall re-routes to KYC-style per-member interviews via Part 1's own machinery. (3) Boundary Manager governs DEPTH (not harm) — earn depth don't take it, follow engagement, back off at withdrawal, never shame a gap, detail must visibly serve the family, sensitivity gates depth; failure mode is creepiness/surveillance-feeling, deliberately distinct from Part 4's failure mode. (4) Safety Layer, DISTINCT from the Boundary Manager, governs HARM — spine: recognize-and-route, never adjudicate, refuse to be weaponized; RESOLVED: capable-person disclosure, false accusation, non-advocating dependent; OPEN/NEEDS-EXPERTS (ethicist+psychologist+attorney gate before ANY build, not even a stub): disclosure about another adult member, non-physical coercion, partially-advocating dependent, jurisdiction-varying mandatory-reporting law, subpoena/data-demand, account-holder-is-abuser, capacity changing over time, consent withdrawn under duress. (5) Cross-Cutting Constraint — operator-custodial hardware, no per-member device keys yet, directly caps what Parts 3-4 can guarantee and is the structural root of Part 4's hardest open item (account-holder-is-abuser); stated once so neither part silently assumes it away. | Confirmation model rethink (portraits not photographs) + meta-governance, answering Fable D-46 1.1/1.2/1.4/1.5 | design/HIP_ConfirmationModel_PortraitRethink__v20260731_0730.md | DESIGN-DRAFT | rebuilds HIP_HouseholdSeeding_Roadmap's Part 1 confirmation model in place (does not replace the roadmap; Parts 3-5 untouched except where THE SEAM section names the Part 1/Part 4 handoff), no code exists. CORE ANALOGY: HIP stores PORTRAITS not PHOTOGRAPHS -- every claim is an attributed rendering ('X asserts Y, based on Z, at time T'), signed by its author, never mistaken for truth about the subject; two disagreeing portraits both kept (Cubist: hold the contradiction); confirmation is the artist signing the canvas, not the museum declaring the portrait is reality. SIX PRINCIPLES: (1) atomic unit = attributed claim; (2) confirmation signs an attribution not a truth, only the authorized subject confirms their own; (3) corroboration is a SEPARATE AXIS from the trust rung, not a rung itself -- Fable 1.1 CONFIRMED: the roadmap's prior 'household agreement -> CORROBORATED' wiring is code-verified BACKWARDS (CORROBORATED=2 ranks below CONFIRMED=3 in memory_engine/trust.py's TRUST_RANK) and structurally unreachable (first-match-wins classification permanently shadows it); the fix needs confirmed_by to become a SET not a scalar, a real schema change, not a doc fix; (4) terminal states replace 'never dead-ends' -- DECLINED/UNREACHABLE/NO_AUTHORITY_RESOLVED/CONTESTED/AUTHOR_ONLY/UNSAFE_TO_CONFIRM -- with the correction (Fable 1.2) that DECLINED cannot be derived from trust rung/confirmed_by/roster (a decline is byte-identical to never-asked in all three) and must be STORED; (5) never proactively route a third-party claim to its subject -- confirmation emerges only when that subject independently volunteers, the direct fix for Fable's finding that Part 2's entire 'People' zone was an instance of Part 4's own gated-unresolved 'disclosure about another adult member' case; (6) CONTENT-BLIND custody -- protection cannot branch on what a claim is about. FIVE STRESS-TEST CASES specified as the actual spec: drinking claim held neutrally with no verdict; music claim as the structural twin proving content-blindness (same code path, diff the trace); subject's own later, differently-framed statement recorded as HIS claim, never treated as confirming the original claimant's verdict; 'he beat me up' as DISCLOSURE where Part 1 neutrality stops and Part 4 takes over; false witness where HIP renders no verdicts, never aggregates multiple unconfirmed claims into a profile or risk score. THE SEAM: the claim-vs-disclosure recognizer is the one hard boundary between Part 1 and Part 4 and MUST FAIL TOWARD SAFETY (ambiguous case -> route to Part 4, never silently absorbed as an ordinary claim); needs psychologist + attorney review before build. ACTIVE-TRUTH-SEEKING FORK RESOLVED: a non-configurable FLOOR (never adjudicate, never register accusations as facts, never aggregate into a profile, attributed-claims-only, harm always routes to Part 4) plus a configurable SWITCH above it (how much truth-seeking scaffolding is offered to the USER, e.g. 'would you like to ask Michael directly') -- switch never subtracts from the floor in either direction and is itself governed by meta-governance so a false accuser cannot weaponize it. FABLE 1.4 (new, SERIOUS, unaddressed by D-47/D-48): confirmation and depth-seeking have OPPOSITE consent polarities (near-zero marginal cost confirming what's already disclosed vs real marginal cost eliciting what hasn't been) so merging them into one queue spends cheaply-earned trust on expensive elicitation; the resulting loop has no damping term and its only brake (withdrawal detection) fails on exactly the population that never signals -- the lonely, compliant, cognitively declining -- so those who most need a ceiling get the highest one; FIX: separate the confirmation queue from the elicitation queue (closes the roadmap's own 'one queue or two' open question -- Fable 1.4 answers it: two, for consent reasons), an absolute depth ceiling engagement can only LOWER never raise, a saturation term. FABLE 1.5 (new): 'never gate help on confirmation' (Part 1) flatly contradicts the MINIMUM SEED degraded-mode message 'I can do more once I know more about X' (Part 2) -- textbook soft-pressure gating despite the invitational framing; one principle must yield, flagged not resolved; also next_confirmable is uncertainty sampling under another name, which surfaces the weirdest/most-sensitive facts first, fighting 'low-friction first' head-on. META-GOVERNANCE named as a sixth foundational area, explicitly NOT designed here: who can change a permission/role and under what process; diagnosis-triggered auto-flip vs pre-declared durable POA as structurally different mechanisms; whether the amendment process can amend itself; authority disputes (conflicting POAs); custodian succession; minor reaching majority; erratic self-rule-changes under capacity decline (meets Part 4 item 7 directly); household dissolution. PRINCIPLE: meta-rules explicit, deterministic, auditable, never improvised by the model at the moment a case arises -- same discipline this codebase already applies to INJ-1..7 and the isolation gate's server-derived-never-caller-asserted provenance. STRATEGIC framing: a COMPOSITION problem, not a novel one -- borrows from OS/DB permission-and-rollback models, IAM roles/delegation, healthcare proxy-consent/auditing standards, legal authority-dispute standards, version-control history, and constitutional-amendment theory (entrenchment: which rules are harder to change than others). Needs its own design pass + attorney review before any of it is built. Grounded directly against the banked reviews (D-49): Fable 1.1/1.2/1.3/1.4/1.5 read from docs/reviews/FABLE_D46_seeding-critique.md before writing, and the drinking/third-party-disclosure/weaponized-queue stress-test material traces to docs/reviews/CHATGPT_research-pass1.txt. Naming Law note: dispatch requested `_v20260731` (no HHMM); filed as `_v20260731_0730` per the Law instead, flagged not bypassed, same as D-45/D-47's handling.
